}
def seed_defaults(db: Session) -> None:
    if db.scalar(select(User).limit(1)) is None:
        db.add(
            User(
                username="admin",
                password_hash=hash_password("123456"),
                full_name="Quản trị viên",
                is_active=True,
                must_change_password=True,
            )
        )
    existing_keys = set(db.scalars(select(Setting.key)).all())
    for key, value in DEFAULT_SETTINGS.items():
        if key not in existing_keys:
            db.add(Setting(key=key, value=value))
    db.commit()

// app/database.py
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, sessionmaker
BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DATABASE_DIR = BASE_DIR / "database"
DATABASE_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
DATABASE_URL = f"sqlite:///{DATABASE_DIR / 'quanlylophoc.sqlite3'}"
engine = create_engine(
    DATABASE_URL,
    connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    future=True,
)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False, future=True)
class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass
def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()
def init_db() -> None:
    from app import models  # noqa: F401
    from sqlalchemy import text
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    with engine.connect() as conn:
        cursor = conn.execute(text("PRAGMA table_info(classes)"))
        columns = [row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()]
        if "teacher_id" not in columns:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE classes ADD COLUMN teacher_id INTEGER REFERENCES teachers(id)"))
        if "salary_type" not in columns:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE classes ADD COLUMN salary_type VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'fixed' NOT NULL"))
        if "fixed_salary_per_session" not in columns:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE classes ADD COLUMN fixed_salary_per_session INTEGER DEFAULT 450000 NOT NULL"))
        if "salary_coefficient" not in columns:
            conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE classes ADD COLUMN salary_coefficient FLOAT DEFAULT 1.0 NOT NULL"))
        rows = conn.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT id, teacher_id, salary_type, fixed_salary_per_session, salary_coefficient "
                "FROM classes WHERE teacher_id IS NOT NULL"
            )
        ).fetchall()
        for row in rows:
            class_id, teacher_id, salary_type, fixed_sal, coeff = row
            existing = conn.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT id FROM teacher_class_assignments "
                    "WHERE class_id = :cid AND teacher_id = :tid"
                ),
                {"cid": class_id, "tid": teacher_id},
            ).fetchone()
            if not existing:
                conn.execute(
                    text(
                        "INSERT INTO teacher_class_assignments "
                        "(class_id, teacher_id, role, salary_type, fixed_salary_per_session, salary_coefficient, is_active) "
                        "VALUES (:cid, :tid, 'main', :stype, :fixed, :coeff, 1)"
                    ),
                    {
                        "cid": class_id,
                        "tid": teacher_id,
                        "stype": salary_type,
                        "fixed": fixed_sal,
                        "coeff": coeff,
                    },
                )
        conn.commit()

// app/main.py
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.auth import get_current_user, get_user_from_request
from app.bootstrap import seed_defaults
from app.database import BASE_DIR, SessionLocal, get_db, init_db
from app.models import User
from app.routers import admin_users, attendance, auth, classes, dashboard, settings, students, tuition, data_management, teachers, payroll, teacher_attendance
from app.timezone import today_vietnam
templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(BASE_DIR / "templates"))
@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    init_db()
    with SessionLocal() as db:
        seed_defaults(db)
    yield
def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI(title="Quản lý lớp học", version="1.0.0", lifespan=lifespan)
    app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(BASE_DIR / "static")), name="static")
    app.include_router(auth.router)
    app.include_router(admin_users.router)
    app.include_router(classes.router)
    app.include_router(students.router)
    app.include_router(students.enrollment_router)
    app.include_router(attendance.router)
    app.include_router(tuition.router)
    app.include_router(settings.router)
    app.include_router(data_management.router)
    app.include_router(teachers.router)
    app.include_router(payroll.router)
    app.include_router(teacher_attendance.router)
    app.include_router(dashboard.router)
    @app.get("/")
    def root() -> RedirectResponse:
        return RedirectResponse(url="/dashboard")
    @app.get("/login")
    def login_page(request: Request):
        return templates.TemplateResponse("login.html", {"request": request})
    def page_context(request: Request, active: str, title: str, user: User, db: Session) -> dict:
        from app.services.settings_service import get_settings_map
        settings = get_settings_map(db)
        return {
            "request": request,
            "active": active,
            "title": title,
            "user": user,
            "today": today_vietnam(),
            "center_logo_text": settings.get("center_logo_text", "Hoa Tuyết\nEducation"),
        }
    def page_user(request: Request, db: Session) -> User | RedirectResponse:
        user = get_user_from_request(request, db)
        if not user:
            return RedirectResponse(url="/login")
        return user
    @app.get("/dashboard")
    def dashboard_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("dashboard.html", page_context(request, "dashboard", "Tổng quan", user, db))
    @app.get("/students")
    def students_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("students.html", page_context(request, "students", "Học sinh", user, db))
    @app.get("/classes")
    def classes_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("classes.html", page_context(request, "classes", "Lớp/Môn học", user, db))
    @app.get("/attendance")
    def attendance_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("attendance.html", page_context(request, "attendance", "Điểm danh", user, db))
    @app.get("/tuition")
    def tuition_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("tuition.html", page_context(request, "tuition", "Học phí", user, db))
    @app.get("/settings")
    def settings_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("settings.html", page_context(request, "settings", "Cài đặt phiếu thu", user, db))
    @app.get("/data-management")
    def data_management_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("data_management.html", page_context(request, "data_management", "Quản lý dữ liệu", user, db))
    @app.get("/center-info")
    def center_info_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("center_info.html", page_context(request, "center_info", "Thông tin trung tâm", user, db))
    @app.get("/admin-users")
    def admin_users_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("admin_users.html", page_context(request, "admin_users", "Tài khoản", user, db))
    @app.get("/teachers")
    def teachers_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("teachers.html", page_context(request, "teachers", "Giáo viên", user, db))
    @app.get("/payroll")
    def payroll_page(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        user = page_user(request, db)
        if isinstance(user, RedirectResponse):
            return user
        return templates.TemplateResponse("payroll.html", page_context(request, "payroll", "Lương giáo viên", user, db))
    return app
app = create_app()

// app/models.py
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import date, datetime
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Date, DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, Float
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
from app.database import Base
from app.timezone import now_vietnam, today_vietnam
class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    password_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    full_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), nullable=False)
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    must_change_password: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, onupdate=now_vietnam, nullable=False
    )
    locked_periods: Mapped[list["TuitionPeriod"]] = relationship(back_populates="locked_by_user")
class Student(Base):
    __tablename__ = "students"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    student_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    full_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), nullable=False)
    parent_phone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40))
    notes: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, onupdate=now_vietnam, nullable=False
    )
    enrollments: Mapped[list["Enrollment"]] = relationship(back_populates="student", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    attendance: Mapped[list["Attendance"]] = relationship(back_populates="student")
    tuition_records: Mapped[list["TuitionRecord"]] = relationship(back_populates="student")
class Teacher(Base):
    __tablename__ = "teachers"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    full_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), nullable=False)
    phone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40))
    email: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120))
    default_salary_coefficient: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=1.0, nullable=False)
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, onupdate=now_vietnam, nullable=False
    )
    classes: Mapped[list["Class"]] = relationship(back_populates="teacher")
    class_assignments: Mapped[list["TeacherClassAssignment"]] = relationship(back_populates="teacher", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    salary_records: Mapped[list["TeacherSalaryRecord"]] = relationship(back_populates="teacher", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    teacher_attendance: Mapped[list["TeacherAttendance"]] = relationship(back_populates="teacher", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
class Class(Base):
    __tablename__ = "classes"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    subject: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    default_fee: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    notes: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, onupdate=now_vietnam, nullable=False
    )
    teacher_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("teachers.id"), nullable=True)
    salary_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="fixed", nullable=False) # "fixed" hoặc "coefficient"
    fixed_salary_per_session: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=450000, nullable=False)
    salary_coefficient: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=1.0, nullable=False)
    teacher: Mapped[Teacher | None] = relationship(back_populates="classes")
    enrollments: Mapped[list["Enrollment"]] = relationship(back_populates="class_")
    attendance: Mapped[list["Attendance"]] = relationship(back_populates="class_")
    assignments: Mapped[list["TeacherClassAssignment"]] = relationship(back_populates="class_", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    teacher_attendance: Mapped[list["TeacherAttendance"]] = relationship(back_populates="class_", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
class TeacherClassAssignment(Base):
    """Phân công giáo viên vào lớp (Many-to-Many) với cấu hình lương riêng."""
    __tablename__ = "teacher_class_assignments"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("class_id", "teacher_id", name="uq_teacher_class_assignment"),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    class_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("classes.id"), nullable=False)
    teacher_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("teachers.id"), nullable=False)
    role: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="main", nullable=False)  # "main" hoặc "assistant"
    salary_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="fixed", nullable=False)
    fixed_salary_per_session: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=450000, nullable=False)
    salary_coefficient: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=1.0, nullable=False)
    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    class_: Mapped[Class] = relationship(back_populates="assignments")
    teacher: Mapped[Teacher] = relationship(back_populates="class_assignments")
class TeacherAttendance(Base):
    """Điểm danh giáo viên, độc lập với điểm danh học sinh."""

    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("teacher_id", "class_id", "date", name="uq_teacher_attendance"),)

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=now_vietnam, nullable=False)
    class_: Mapped[Class] = relationship(back_populates="teacher_attendance")

    calculated_amount: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
    record: Mapped[TeacherSalaryRecord] = relationship(back_populates="items")
    class_: Mapped[Class | None] = relationship()

// app/schemas.py
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import date
from typing import Literal
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
AttendanceStatus = Literal["P", "V", "M"]
class UserCreate(BaseModel):
    username: str = Field(min_length=3, max_length=80)
    password: str = Field(min_length=6)
    full_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    is_active: bool = True
class UserUpdate(BaseModel):
    full_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    is_active: bool = True
class PasswordUpdate(BaseModel):
    password: str = Field(min_length=6)
class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    password: str
class TeacherAssignmentIn(BaseModel):
    teacher_id: int
    role: Literal["main", "assistant"] = "main"
    salary_type: Literal["fixed", "coefficient"] = "fixed"
    fixed_salary_per_session: int = Field(default=450000, ge=0)
    salary_coefficient: float = Field(default=1.0, ge=0.0)
class ClassCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    subject: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    default_fee: int = Field(ge=0)
    notes: str | None = None
    is_active: bool = True
    teacher_id: int | None = None
    salary_type: Literal["fixed", "coefficient"] = "fixed"
    fixed_salary_per_session: int = Field(default=450000, ge=0)
    salary_coefficient: float = Field(default=1.0, ge=0.0)
    assignments: list[TeacherAssignmentIn] = []
class ClassUpdate(ClassCreate):
    pass
class StudentCreate(BaseModel):
    student_code: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=80)
    full_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    parent_phone: str | None = None
    notes: str | None = None
    is_active: bool = True
class StudentUpdate(BaseModel):
    student_code: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=80)
    full_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    parent_phone: str | None = None
    notes: str | None = None
    is_active: bool = True
class EnrollmentCreate(BaseModel):
    student_id: int
    class_ids: list[int]
    custom_fee: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=0)
    is_exempt: bool = False
    start_date: date | None = None
    is_active: bool = True
    notes: str | None = None
class EnrollmentUpdate(BaseModel):
    custom_fee: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=0)
    is_exempt: bool = False
    start_date: date | None = None
    is_active: bool = True
    notes: str | None = None
class AttendanceItem(BaseModel):
    student_id: int
    status: AttendanceStatus
class AttendanceBulkSave(BaseModel):
    class_id: int
    date: date
    items: list[AttendanceItem]
class TuitionLockRequest(BaseModel):
    month: int = Field(ge=1, le=12)
    year: int = Field(ge=2000, le=2100)
    class_id: int | None = None
class SettingUpdate(BaseModel):
    key: str
    value: str
class ORMModel(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)
class UserOut(ORMModel):
    id: int
    username: str
    full_name: str
    is_active: bool
    must_change_password: bool
class TeacherAssignmentOut(ORMModel):
    id: int
    teacher_id: int
    role: str
    salary_type: str
    fixed_salary_per_session: int
    salary_coefficient: float
    is_active: bool
class ClassOut(ORMModel):
    id: int
    name: str
    subject: str
    default_fee: int
    notes: str | None
    is_active: bool
    teacher_id: int | None = None
    salary_type: str = "fixed"
    fixed_salary_per_session: int = 450000
    salary_coefficient: float = 1.0
    assignments: list[TeacherAssignmentOut] = []
class StudentOut(ORMModel):
    id: int
    student_code: str
    full_name: str
    parent_phone: str | None
    notes: str | None
    is_active: bool
class TeacherCreate(BaseModel):
    full_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    phone: str | None = None
    email: str | None = None
    default_salary_coefficient: float = Field(default=1.0, ge=0.0)
    is_active: bool = True
class TeacherUpdate(BaseModel):
    full_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    phone: str | None = None
    email: str | None = None
    default_salary_coefficient: float = Field(default=1.0, ge=0.0)
    is_active: bool = True
class TeacherOut(ORMModel):
    id: int
    full_name: str
    phone: str | None
    email: str | None
    default_salary_coefficient: float
    is_active: bool
class PayrollLockRequest(BaseModel):
    month: int = Field(ge=1, le=12)
    year: int = Field(ge=2000, le=2100)

// app/timezone.py
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone, timedelta
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo, ZoneInfoNotFoundError
try:
    VIETNAM_TZ = ZoneInfo("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh")
except ZoneInfoNotFoundError:
    VIETNAM_TZ = timezone(timedelta(hours=7), name="Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh")
def now_vietnam() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(VIETNAM_TZ)
def today_vietnam() -> date:
    return now_vietnam().date()
def parse_local_date(value: str) -> date:
    return date.fromisoformat(value)
def month_bounds(year: int, month: int) -> tuple[date, date]:
    start = date(year, month, 1)
    if month == 12:
        end = date(year + 1, 1, 1)
    else:
        end = date(year, month + 1, 1)
    return start, end

// static/js/app.js
const formatter = new Intl.NumberFormat("vi-VN");
function formatMoney(value) {
  return formatter.format(Number(value || 0));
}
function todayIso() {
  const now = new Date();
  const offset = now.getTimezoneOffset();
  const local = new Date(now.getTime() - offset * 60000);
  return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}
async function api(url, options = {}) {
  const headers = options.body instanceof FormData ? {} : { "Content-Type": "application/json" };
  const response = await fetch(url, { credentials: "same-origin", headers, ...options });
  const type = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
  const data = type.includes("application/json") ? await response.json() : await response.text();
  if (!response.ok) {
    const message = typeof data === "object" ? data.detail || data.message : data;
    throw new Error(message || "Có lỗi xảy ra, vui lòng thử lại.");
  }
  return data;
}
function toast(message, isError = false) {
  const node = document.createElement("div");
  node.className = `toast ${isError ? "error" : ""}`;
  node.textContent = message;
  document.body.appendChild(node);
  setTimeout(() => node.remove(), 3200);
}
function formDataJson(form) {
  const formData = new FormData(form);
  const data = {};
  for (const name of formData.keys()) {
    const element = form.elements[name];
    if (element && element.multiple) {
      const values = formData.getAll(name);
      const isNumber = element.dataset.number === "true" || element.getAttribute("data-number") === "true";
      data[name] = isNumber ? values.map(Number) : values;
    } else {
      data[name] = formData.get(name);
    }
  }
  for (const input of form.querySelectorAll("input[type='checkbox']")) {
    data[input.name] = input.checked;
  }
  for (const input of form.querySelectorAll("[data-number='true']:not(select)")) {
    data[input.name] = input.value === "" ? null : Number(input.value);
  }
  return data;
}
function requireConfirm(message) {
  return window.confirm(message);
}
async function logout() {
  await api("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" });
  window.location.href = "/login";
}
function setText(id, value) {
  const node = document.getElementById(id);
  if (node) node.textContent = value;
}
async function checkAndRunAutoBackup() {
  if (window.location.pathname === "/login") {
    return;
  }
  if (sessionStorage.getItem("auto_backup_checked") === "true") {
    return;
  }
  try {
    const status = await api("/api/data/check-auto-backup");
    if (status.due) {
      toast("Đang tự động tải bản sao lưu định kỳ của hệ thống...");
      const response = await fetch("/api/data/export", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          targets: ["students", "classes", "attendance", "tuition", "settings", "users"],
          format: "sql"
        })
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error("Không thể tải file sao lưu.");
      }
      const blob = await response.blob();
      const contentDisposition = response.headers.get('Content-Disposition');
      let filename = `backup_auto_${status.backup_date}.sql`;
      if (contentDisposition && contentDisposition.indexOf('filename=') !== -1) {
        const matches = contentDisposition.match(/filename="([^"]+)"/);
        if (matches != null && matches[1]) {
          filename = matches[1];
        }
      }
      const link = document.createElement("a");
      link.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      link.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      await api("/api/data/confirm-auto-backup", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ backup_date: status.backup_date })
      });
      toast(`Đã tự động tải về bản sao lưu ngày ${status.backup_date} thành công.`);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Auto-backup check failed:", err);
  } finally {
    sessionStorage.setItem("auto_backup_checked", "true");
  }
}
if (document.readyState === "loading") {
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    checkAndRunAutoBackup().catch(console.error);
  });
} else {
  checkAndRunAutoBackup().catch(console.error);
}

        <textarea class="field rounded-lg border-slate-200" name="center_address" rows="2" required></textarea>
      </div>
      <div class="md:col-span-2 flex justify-end">
        <button class="btn btn-primary py-2.5 px-6 rounded-lg font-bold">Lưu thông tin</button>
      </div>

{% block scripts %}
<script>
  const fieldNames = [
    "center_name",
    "center_hotline",
    "center_phone",
    "center_email",
    "center_zalo",
    "center_address"
  ];
  async function loadCenterSettings() {
    const settings = await api("/api/settings");
    const form = document.getElementById("centerInfoForm");
    for (const name of fieldNames) {
      if (form.elements[name]) {
        form.elements[name].value = settings[name] || "";
      }
    }
  }
  document.getElementById("centerInfoForm").addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const payload = fieldNames.map((key) => ({
      key,
      value: String(event.target.elements[key]?.value || ""),
    }));
    await api("/api/settings", { method: "PUT", body: JSON.stringify(payload) });
    toast("Đã lưu thông tin trung tâm thành công.");
  });
  loadCenterSettings().catch((err) => toast(err.message, true));
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
  // 1. Thao tác hiển thị tên file được chọn
  document.getElementById('importSqlFile').addEventListener('change', function(e) {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    const label = document.getElementById('importFileLabel');
    if (file) {
      label.textContent = file.name + ' (' + (file.size / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB)';
    } else {
      label.textContent = 'Chọn tập tin backup .sql';
    }
  });
  // 2. Thao tác gửi yêu cầu kết xuất dữ liệu
  document.getElementById('exportConfigForm').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // Thu thập danh sách check-box được chọn
    const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="targets"]:checked');
    const targets = Array.from(checkboxes).map(cb => cb.value);
    if (targets.length === 0) {
      toast("Vui lòng lựa chọn ít nhất một phân hệ dữ liệu cần xuất.", true);
      return;
    }
    const format = document.querySelector('input[name="format"]:checked').value;
    try {
      toast("Đang khởi tạo tiến trình kết xuất dữ liệu...");
      const response = await fetch('/api/data/export', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ targets, format })
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        const errData = await response.json();
        throw new Error(errData.detail || "Không thể thực hiện kết xuất dữ liệu.");
      }
      // Đọc luồng nhị phân và kích hoạt tải xuống
      const blob = await response.blob();
      const contentDisposition = response.headers.get('Content-Disposition');
      let filename = "export_data." + (format === 'excel' ? 'xlsx' : format === 'csv_zip' ? 'zip' : 'sql');
      if (contentDisposition && contentDisposition.indexOf('filename=') !== -1) {
        const matches = contentDisposition.match(/filename="([^"]+)"/);
        if (matches != null && matches[1]) {
          filename = matches[1];
        }
      }
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      link.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      toast("Kết xuất và tải xuống tập tin dữ liệu thành công.");
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Lỗi kết xuất: " + err.message, true);
    }
  });
  // 3. Thao tác gửi yêu cầu nhập/phục hồi dữ liệu
  document.getElementById('importConfigForm').addEventListener('submit', async function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('importSqlFile');
    const file = fileInput.files[0];
    if (!file) {
      toast("Vui lòng tải tập tin .sql lên trước khi thực hiện phục hồi.", true);
      return;
    }
    const confirmed = requireConfirm(
      "Cảnh báo: Quy trình này sẽ thay thế cấu trúc dữ liệu hiện tại bằng dữ liệu của tệp sao lưu. " +
      "Bạn có thực sự muốn tiến hành phục hồi dữ liệu không?"
    );
    if (!confirmed) return;
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    try {
      toast("Đang tiến hành giải mã và khôi phục dữ liệu...");
      const response = await fetch('/api/data/import', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: formData // Gửi dưới dạng Multipart Form Data
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(result.detail || "Quá trình phục hồi dữ liệu thất bại.");
      }
      toast("Phục hồi hoàn tất. Hệ thống đã đồng bộ " + result.details.executed_inserts + " bản ghi.");
      // Tải lại trang sau một thời gian ngắn để đồng bộ giao diện
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.location.reload();
      }, 1500);
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Lỗi khôi phục: " + err.message, true);
    }
  });
  // 4. Thao tác load và lưu cấu hình tự động sao lưu
  async function loadAutoBackupSettings() {
    const settings = await api("/api/settings");
    const daySelect = document.getElementById("backup_schedule_day");
    if (daySelect && settings.backup_schedule_day) {
      daySelect.value = settings.backup_schedule_day;
    }
  }
  document.getElementById("autoBackupForm").addEventListener("submit", async function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const daySelect = document.getElementById("backup_schedule_day");
    const payload = [
      { key: "backup_schedule_day", value: daySelect.value }
    ];
    try {
      await api("/api/settings", {
        method: "PUT",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      toast("Đã lưu thiết lập tự động sao lưu định kỳ.");
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Lỗi khi lưu thiết lập: " + err.message, true);
    }
  });
  // Chạy khi khởi động trang
  loadAutoBackupSettings().catch(err => toast(err.message, true));
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
  let payrollData = [];
  let isLocked = false;
  async function fetchPayroll() {
    const month = document.getElementById("payrollMonth").value;
    const year = document.getElementById("payrollYear").value;
    if (!month || !year) return;
    try {
      const data = await api(`/api/payroll/preview?month=${month}&year=${year}`);
      payrollData = data.records;
      isLocked = data.is_locked;
      document.getElementById("lockedNotice").classList.toggle("hidden", !isLocked);
      const lockBtn = document.getElementById("lockBtn");
      if (isLocked) {
        lockBtn.classList.add("hidden");
      } else {
        lockBtn.classList.remove("hidden");
      }
      renderPayrollTable();
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  }
  function renderPayrollTable() {
    const rows = document.getElementById("payrollRows");
    let totalClassesCount = 0;
    let totalSessionsCount = 0;
    let totalRevenueSum = 0;
    let totalSalarySum = 0;
    if (payrollData.length === 0) {
      rows.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="7" class="text-slate-500 py-8 text-center">Không có dữ liệu giáo viên hoặc tính lương trong kỳ này.</td></tr>`;
      document.getElementById("totalClasses").textContent = "-";
      document.getElementById("totalSessions").textContent = "-";
      document.getElementById("totalRevenue").textContent = "-";
      document.getElementById("totalSalary").textContent = "-";
      return;
    }
    rows.innerHTML = payrollData.map(p => {
      const classesCount = p.classes.length;
      const sessionsCount = p.classes.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.sessions, 0);
      const revenueSum = p.classes.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.revenue, 0);
      totalClassesCount += classesCount;
      totalSessionsCount += sessionsCount;
      totalRevenueSum += revenueSum;
      totalSalarySum += p.total_salary;
      const statusBadge = isLocked 
        ? `<span class="inline-block bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold border border-teal-100">Đã chốt</span>`
        : `<span class="inline-block bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold border border-amber-100">Tạm tính</span>`;
      // Nút in PDF hoặc in phiếu tạm
      const pdfBtn = isLocked 
        ? `<button class="btn btn-primary !min-height-0 !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold" onclick="downloadPayrollPdf(${p.teacher_id})">In phiếu lương</button>`
        : `<button class="btn border border-slate-200 !min-height-0 !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs text-teal-700 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 hover:border-teal-200 font-semibold" onclick="downloadPayrollPdf(${p.teacher_id})">In phiếu tạm</button>`;
      return `
        <tr>
          <td class="font-bold text-slate-800">${p.teacher_name}</td>
          <td class="text-center font-semibold text-slate-600">${classesCount}</td>
          <td class="text-center font-mono">${sessionsCount}</td>
          <td class="text-right font-mono text-slate-700">${formatMoney(revenueSum)}</td>
          <td class="text-right font-mono font-bold text-slate-800">${formatMoney(p.total_salary)}</td>
          <td class="text-center">${statusBadge}</td>
          <td class="text-center">
            <div class="flex justify-center gap-1.5">
              <button class="btn btn-muted border border-slate-200 !min-height-0 !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold" onclick="viewDetails(${p.teacher_id})">Chi tiết</button>
              ${pdfBtn}
            </div>
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join("");
    document.getElementById("totalClasses").textContent = totalClassesCount;
    document.getElementById("totalSessions").textContent = totalSessionsCount;
    document.getElementById("totalRevenue").textContent = formatMoney(totalRevenueSum);
    document.getElementById("totalSalary").textContent = formatMoney(totalSalarySum);
  }
  function viewDetails(teacherId) {
    const teacher = payrollData.find(p => p.teacher_id === teacherId);
    if (!teacher) return;
    document.getElementById("detailTeacherName").textContent = teacher.teacher_name;
    const month = document.getElementById("payrollMonth").value;
    const year = document.getElementById("payrollYear").value;
    document.getElementById("detailPeriod").textContent = `Tháng ${month}/${year}`;
    const rows = document.getElementById("detailRows");
    rows.innerHTML = teacher.classes.map(c => {
      const typeStr = c.salary_type === "fixed" ? "Lương cứng" : "Doanh thu";
      const rateStr = c.salary_type === "fixed" ? formatMoney(c.applied_rate) : c.applied_rate.toFixed(2);
      const revStr = c.salary_type === "fixed" ? "-" : formatMoney(c.revenue);
      return `
        <tr>
          <td class="font-bold text-slate-800">${c.class_name}</td>
          <td class="text-center font-mono">${c.sessions}</td>
          <td class="text-right font-mono">${revStr}</td>
          <td>${typeStr}</td>
          <td class="font-mono text-center">${rateStr}</td>
          <td class="text-right font-mono font-bold text-slate-800">${formatMoney(c.amount)}</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join("");
    document.getElementById("detailModal").classList.remove("hidden");
  }
  function closeDetailModal() {
    document.getElementById("detailModal").classList.add("hidden");
  }
  async function lockPayroll() {
    const month = document.getElementById("payrollMonth").value;
    const year = document.getElementById("payrollYear").value;
    if (!confirm(`Bạn có chắc chắn muốn chốt khóa bảng lương Tháng ${month}/${year}? Sau khi chốt, dữ liệu tính toán sẽ được khóa lịch sử cố định.`)) return;
    try {
      await api("/api/payroll/lock", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          month: parseInt(month),
          year: parseInt(year)
        })
      });
      toast("Đã chốt khóa bảng lương kỳ thành công.");
      fetchPayroll();
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  }
  function downloadPayrollPdf(teacherId) {
    const month = document.getElementById("payrollMonth").value;
    const year = document.getElementById("payrollYear").value;
    if (!month || !year) return;
    window.open(`/api/payroll/export-pdf?month=${month}&year=${year}&teacher_id=${teacherId}`, "_blank");
  }
  function exportAllPayrollPdf() {
    const month = document.getElementById("payrollMonth").value;
    const year = document.getElementById("payrollYear").value;
    if (!month || !year) return;
    window.open(`/api/payroll/export-pdf?month=${month}&year=${year}`, "_blank");
  }
  // Khởi chạy
  fetchPayroll();
</script>
{% endblock %}

        </tbody>
      </table>
      <!-- Signatures -->
      <div class="signatures">
        <div class="signature-block">
          <div class="signature-title">Giáo viên ký nhận</div>
          <div style="font-style: italic; color: #888; font-size: 12px;">(Ký và ghi rõ họ tên)</div>
        </div>
        <div class="signature-block">
          <div class="signature-title">Người lập phiếu</div>
          <div style="font-style: italic; color: #888; font-size: 12px;">(Ký và ghi rõ họ tên)</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    {% endfor %}
  </body>
</html>

// templates/teachers.html
<!-- templates/teachers.html -->
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% block content %}
<div class="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[1fr_360px]">
  <!-- Left Column: Teacher List -->
  <section class="panel p-6 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-100">
    <div class="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3 mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
      <div>
        <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-800">Danh sách Giáo viên</h2>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Quản lý hồ sơ giáo viên và thông số lương mặc định.</p>
      </div>
      <button class="btn btn-muted border border-slate-200" onclick="resetForm()">
        Làm mới danh sách
      </button>
    </div>
    <div class="mt-4 overflow-auto">
      <table id="teachersTable" class="table w-full">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th class="font-bold text-slate-700">Họ tên</th>
            <th class="font-bold text-slate-700">Liên hệ</th>
            <th class="font-bold text-slate-700">Hệ số mặc định</th>
            <th class="font-bold text-slate-700">Lớp phụ trách</th>
            <th class="font-bold text-slate-700">Trạng thái</th>
            <th class="font-bold text-slate-700">Thao tác</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="teacherRows"></tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </section>

    <div class="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3 mb-4">
      <h2 id="formTitle" class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-800">Thêm giáo viên mới</h2>

      </div>

      </div>

          <input id="isActiveCheckbox" name="is_active" type="checkbox" checked class="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500" /> Đang làm việc
        </label>
      </div>
      <div class="flex gap-2 pt-2">
        <button id="submitBtn" class="btn btn-primary flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-lg font-bold">Lưu hồ sơ</button>
        <button type="button" id="cancelBtn" class="btn btn-muted border border-slate-200 hidden" onclick="resetForm()">Hủy</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  </section>

{% block scripts %}
<script>
  let dataTableInstance = null;
  async function loadTeachers() {
    try {
      const teachers = await api("/api/teachers");
      const rows = document.getElementById("teacherRows");
      if (dataTableInstance) {
        dataTableInstance.destroy();
      }
      rows.innerHTML = teachers.map(t => {
        const classBadges = t.classes.length > 0 
          ? t.classes.map(c => `<span class="inline-block bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-teal-100 font-semibold">${c.name}</span>`).join(" ") 
          : `<span class="text-slate-400 text-xs">-</span>`;
        const statusBadge = t.is_active 
          ? `<span class="inline-block bg-green-50 text-green-700 text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">Hoạt động</span>` 
          : `<span class="inline-block bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">Đã nghỉ</span>`;
        return `
          <tr>
            <td class="font-bold text-slate-800">${t.full_name}</td>
            <td>
              <div class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">${t.phone || "-"}</div>
              <div class="text-xs text-slate-400">${t.email || "-"}</div>
            </td>
            <td class="font-mono text-center">${t.default_salary_coefficient.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">${classBadges}</div></td>
            <td class="text-center">${statusBadge}</td>
            <td class="text-center">
              <div class="flex justify-center gap-1.5">
                <button class="btn btn-muted py-1 px-2.5 text-xs border border-slate-200" onclick="editTeacher(${JSON.stringify(t).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')})">Sửa</button>
                <button class="btn btn-muted py-1 px-2.5 text-xs text-red-600 border border-red-100 hover:bg-red-50" onclick="deleteTeacher(${t.id})">Xóa</button>
              </div>
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }).join("");
      dataTableInstance = $("#teachersTable").DataTable({
        language: {
          url: "https://cdn.datatables.net/plug-ins/1.13.7/i18n/vi.json"
        },
        pageLength: 10,
        lengthChange: false,
        ordering: true,
        info: false
      });
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  }
  function editTeacher(t) {
    document.getElementById("formTitle").textContent = "Cập nhật giáo viên";
    document.getElementById("formSubtitle").textContent = "Chỉnh sửa thông tin giáo viên hiện tại.";
    document.getElementById("teacherId").value = t.id;
    const form = document.getElementById("teacherForm");
    form.elements["full_name"].value = t.full_name;
    form.elements["phone"].value = t.phone || "";
    form.elements["email"].value = t.email || "";
    form.elements["default_salary_coefficient"].value = t.default_salary_coefficient;
    document.getElementById("isActiveCheckbox").checked = t.is_active;
    document.getElementById("cancelBtn").classList.remove("hidden");
    document.getElementById("submitBtn").textContent = "Cập nhật";
  }
  function resetForm() {
    document.getElementById("formTitle").textContent = "Thêm giáo viên mới";
    document.getElementById("formSubtitle").textContent = "Đăng ký thêm hồ sơ giáo viên giảng dạy.";
    document.getElementById("teacherId").value = "";
    document.getElementById("teacherForm").reset();
    document.getElementById("isActiveCheckbox").checked = true;
    document.getElementById("cancelBtn").classList.add("hidden");
    document.getElementById("submitBtn").textContent = "Lưu hồ sơ";
  }
  document.getElementById("teacherForm").addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const id = document.getElementById("teacherId").value;
    const form = event.target;
    const payload = {
      full_name: form.elements["full_name"].value.trim(),
      phone: form.elements["phone"].value.trim() || null,
      email: form.elements["email"].value.trim() || null,
      default_salary_coefficient: parseFloat(form.elements["default_salary_coefficient"].value),
      is_active: document.getElementById("isActiveCheckbox").checked
    };
    try {
      if (id) {
        await api(`/api/teachers/${id}`, {
          method: "PUT",
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        toast("Đã cập nhật thông tin giáo viên.");
      } else {
        await api("/api/teachers", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
        toast("Đã thêm giáo viên mới thành công.");
      }
      resetForm();
      loadTeachers();
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  });
  async function deleteTeacher(id) {
    if (!confirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn ngừng hoạt động giáo viên này?")) return;
    try {
      await api(`/api/teachers/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      toast("Đã ngừng hoạt động giáo viên.");
      loadTeachers();
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  }
  // Khởi chạy
  loadTeachers();
</script>
{% endblock %}

    <div class="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3 mb-4">
      <h2 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-800">Tạo tài khoản mới</h2>
      <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Đăng ký thêm tài khoản quản trị mới.</p>

      <div>
        <label class="label text-slate-700 font-semibold">Tên đăng nhập</label>
        <input class="field rounded-lg border-slate-200" name="username" placeholder="Ví dụ: admin2" required />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label class="label text-slate-700 font-semibold">Họ tên</label>
        <input class="field rounded-lg border-slate-200" name="full_name" placeholder="Ví dụ: Nguyễn Văn B" required />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label class="label text-slate-700 font-semibold">Mật khẩu</label>
        <input class="field rounded-lg border-slate-200" name="password" type="password" required minlength="6" placeholder="Tối thiểu 6 ký tự" />
      </div>
      <div class="pt-1">
        <label class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 py-1">
          <input name="is_active" type="checkbox" checked class="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500" /> Đang hoạt động hoạt động
        </label>
      </div>
      <button class="btn btn-primary w-full py-2.5 rounded-lg font-bold">Tạo tài khoản</button>
    </form>
  </section>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block scripts %}
<script>
  let dataTableInstance = null;
  async function loadUsers() {
    const users = await api("/api/admin/users");
    if (dataTableInstance) {
      dataTableInstance.destroy();
    }
    document.getElementById("userRows").innerHTML = users.map((u) => `
      <tr>
        <td class="font-bold text-teal-800 align-middle">${u.username}</td>
        <td class="align-middle"><input class="field rounded-lg border-slate-200 !min-height-0 !py-1.5" id="name-${u.id}" value="${u.full_name}" /></td>
        <td class="align-middle">
          <label class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 py-1">
            <input id="active-${u.id}" type="checkbox" class="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500" ${u.is_active ? "checked" : ""} /> Đang hoạt động
          </label>
        </td>
        <td class="space-y-2">
          <input class="field rounded-lg border-slate-200 !min-height-0 !py-1.5" id="pass-${u.id}" type="password" placeholder="Mật khẩu mới" />
          <div class="flex gap-1.5">
            <button class="btn btn-muted !min-height-0 !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold" onclick="saveUser(${u.id})">Lưu</button>
            <button class="btn btn-primary !min-height-0 !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold" onclick="savePassword(${u.id})">Đổi mật khẩu</button>
          </div>
        </td>
      </tr>
    `).join("");
    dataTableInstance = $("#usersTable").DataTable({
      language: {
        search: "Tìm kiếm:",
        lengthMenu: "Hiển thị _MENU_ tài khoản",
        info: "Đang xem _START_ đến _END_ trong tổng số _TOTAL_ tài khoản",
        paginate: {
          first: "Đầu",
          last: "Cuối",
          next: "Tiếp",
          previous: "Trước"
        }
      },
      pageLength: 10,
      order: [[0, "asc"]],
      retrieve: true
    });
  }
  async function saveUser(id) {
    await api(`/api/admin/users/${id}`, { 
      method: "PUT", 
      body: JSON.stringify({ 
        full_name: document.getElementById(`name-${id}`).value, 
        is_active: document.getElementById(`active-${id}`).checked 
      }) 
    });
    toast("Đã lưu tài khoản.");
    loadUsers();
  }
  async function savePassword(id) {
    const password = document.getElementById(`pass-${id}`).value;
    if (!password || password.length < 6) return toast("Mật khẩu mới cần ít nhất 6 ký tự.", true);
    await api(`/api/admin/users/${id}/password`, { method: "PUT", body: JSON.stringify({ password }) });
    toast("Đã đổi mật khẩu.");
    loadUsers();
  }
  document.getElementById("userForm").addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    await api("/api/admin/users", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(formDataJson(event.target)) });
    event.target.reset();
    event.target.elements.is_active.checked = true;
    toast("Đã tạo tài khoản.");
    loadUsers();
  });
  loadUsers().catch((err) => toast(err.message, true));
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
  let isLocked = false;
  let currentYear = {{ today.year }};
  let currentMonth = {{ today.month }};
  let currentTab = "student";
  function getDaysInMonth(month, year) {
    return new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
  }
  function switchTab(tab) {
    currentTab = tab;
    const btnStudent = document.getElementById("btnTabStudent");
    const btnTeacher = document.getElementById("btnTabTeacher");
    const title = document.getElementById("attendanceTitle");
    if (tab === "student") {
      btnStudent.className = "px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-teal-600 text-teal-600 transition";
      btnTeacher.className = "px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 transition";
      title.textContent = "Điểm danh Học sinh";
    } else {
      btnStudent.className = "px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700 transition";
      btnTeacher.className = "px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-teal-600 text-teal-600 transition";
      title.textContent = "Điểm danh Giáo viên";
    }
    localStorage.setItem("lastAttendanceTab", tab);
    updateLegend();
    fetchGrid();
  }
  function updateLegend() {
    const legend = document.getElementById("attendanceLegend");
    if (currentTab === "student") {
      legend.innerHTML = `
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <input type="checkbox" checked disabled class="attendance-checkbox" style="width: 16px; height: 16px; accent-color: #0F766E;" />
          <span class="text-slate-600">Có mặt / Đi học muộn</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <input type="checkbox" disabled class="attendance-checkbox" style="width: 16px; height: 16px; accent-color: #0F766E;" />
          <span class="text-slate-600">Vắng mặt / Chưa điểm danh</span>
        </div>
      `;
    } else {
      legend.innerHTML = `
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="teacher-att-cell teacher-att-P text-center" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; line-height: 18px; font-size: 11px;">P</span>
          <span class="text-slate-600">Có mặt</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="teacher-att-cell teacher-att-V text-center" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; line-height: 18px; font-size: 11px;">V</span>
          <span class="text-slate-600">Vắng mặt</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="teacher-att-cell teacher-att-M text-center" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; line-height: 18px; font-size: 11px;">M</span>
          <span class="text-slate-600">Đi muộn</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span class="teacher-att-cell teacher-att-empty text-center" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; line-height: 18px; font-size: 11px;">-</span>
          <span class="text-slate-600">Chưa điểm danh</span>
        </div>
      `;
    }
  }
  async function loadClasses() {
    const classes = await api("/api/classes?active=true");
    const select = document.getElementById("attendanceClass");
    select.innerHTML = classes.map((c) => `<option value="${c.id}">${c.name} - ${c.subject}</option>`).join("");
    // Khôi phục state từ localStorage
    const savedClass = localStorage.getItem("lastAttendanceClassId");
    if (savedClass && classes.some(c => c.id == savedClass)) {
      select.value = savedClass;
    }
    // Khởi tạo Select2
    $("#attendanceClass").select2({
      placeholder: "Gõ để tìm kiếm lớp...",
      width: '100%'
    });
  }
  function initFilters() {
    const monthInput = document.getElementById("attendanceMonth");
    const yearInput = document.getElementById("attendanceYear");
    const savedMonth = localStorage.getItem("lastAttendanceMonth");
    const savedYear = localStorage.getItem("lastAttendanceYear");
    monthInput.value = savedMonth ? savedMonth : currentMonth;
    yearInput.value = savedYear ? savedYear : currentYear;
    // Lắng nghe sự kiện thay đổi để load lại Grid
    $("#attendanceClass").on("change", fetchGrid);
    monthInput.addEventListener("change", fetchGrid);
    yearInput.addEventListener("change", fetchGrid);
  }
  async function fetchGrid() {
    const classId = document.getElementById("attendanceClass").value;
    const month = document.getElementById("attendanceMonth").value;
    const year = document.getElementById("attendanceYear").value;
    if (!classId || !month || !year) return;
    // Lưu state lại
    localStorage.setItem("lastAttendanceClassId", classId);
    localStorage.setItem("lastAttendanceMonth", month);
    localStorage.setItem("lastAttendanceYear", year);
    try {
      if (currentTab === "student") {
        const data = await api(`/api/attendance/month?class_id=${classId}&month=${month}&year=${year}`);
        isLocked = data.is_locked;
        document.getElementById("lockedNotice").classList.toggle("hidden", !isLocked);
        renderStudentGrid(data.students, month, year, classId);
      } else {
        const data = await api(`/api/teacher-attendance/month?class_id=${classId}&month=${month}&year=${year}`);
        document.getElementById("lockedNotice").classList.add("hidden");
        renderTeacherGrid(data.teachers, month, year, classId);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  }
  function renderStudentGrid(students, month, year, classId) {
    const days = getDaysInMonth(month, year);
    const head = document.getElementById("gridHead");
    const body = document.getElementById("gridBody");
    // Render Header
    let headHtml = `<tr><th class="sticky-col font-bold text-slate-800">Học sinh</th>`;
    for (let i = 1; i <= days; i++) {
      headHtml += `<th class="text-slate-600 font-bold">${i}</th>`;
    }
    headHtml += `</tr>`;
    head.innerHTML = headHtml;
    // Render Body
    if (students.length === 0) {
      body.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="${days + 1}" class="text-slate-500 py-8 text-center">Chưa có học sinh trong lớp này.</td></tr>`;
      return;
    }
    let bodyHtml = "";
    students.forEach(student => {
      bodyHtml += `<tr><td class="sticky-col">
          <div class="font-bold text-slate-800">${student.full_name}</div>
          <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">${student.student_code}</div>
        </td>`;
      for (let day = 1; day <= days; day++) {
        const dateStr = `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        const currentStatus = student.attendance[dateStr] || "";
        const isChecked = (currentStatus === "P" || currentStatus === "M") ? "checked" : "";
        bodyHtml += `<td>
          <input 
            type="checkbox"
            id="chk_${student.student_id}_${dateStr}"
            class="attendance-checkbox" 
            ${isChecked}
            onchange="toggleCheckboxStatus(this, ${student.student_id}, ${classId}, '${dateStr}')"
          />
        </td>`;
      }
      bodyHtml += `</tr>`;
    });
    body.innerHTML = bodyHtml;
  }
  async function toggleCheckboxStatus(checkbox, studentId, classId, dateStr) {
    const isChecked = checkbox.checked;
    const nextStatus = isChecked ? "P" : "V";
    checkbox.disabled = true;
    try {
      await api("/api/attendance/single", {
        method: "PUT",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          class_id: classId,
          student_id: studentId,
          date: dateStr,
          status: nextStatus
        })
      });
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Lỗi khi lưu: " + err.message, true);
      checkbox.checked = !isChecked;
    } finally {
      checkbox.disabled = false;
    }
  }
  function renderTeacherGrid(teachers, month, year, classId) {
    const days = getDaysInMonth(month, year);
    const head = document.getElementById("gridHead");
    const body = document.getElementById("gridBody");
    // Render Header
    let headHtml = `<tr><th class="sticky-col font-bold text-slate-800">Giáo viên</th>`;
    for (let i = 1; i <= days; i++) {
      headHtml += `<th class="text-slate-600 font-bold">${i}</th>`;
    }
    headHtml += `</tr>`;
    head.innerHTML = headHtml;
    // Render Body
    if (teachers.length === 0) {
      body.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="${days + 1}" class="text-slate-500 py-8 text-center">Chưa có giáo viên được phân công vào lớp này.</td></tr>`;
      return;
    }
    let bodyHtml = "";
    teachers.forEach(teacher => {
      const roleBadge = teacher.role === "main"
        ? `<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 rounded border border-teal-200 ml-1">Chính</span>`
        : `<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 rounded border border-slate-200 ml-1">Phụ</span>`;
      bodyHtml += `<tr><td class="sticky-col">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span class="font-bold text-slate-800">${teacher.full_name}</span>
            ${roleBadge}
          </div>
        </td>`;
      for (let day = 1; day <= days; day++) {
        const dateStr = `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        const currentStatus = teacher.attendance[dateStr] || "";
        bodyHtml += `<td>
          <div 
            data-status="${currentStatus}"
            class="teacher-att-cell ${currentStatus ? 'teacher-att-' + currentStatus : 'teacher-att-empty'} text-center"
            onclick="cycleTeacherAttendance(this, ${teacher.teacher_id}, ${classId}, '${dateStr}')"
          >${currentStatus ? currentStatus : "-"}</div>
        </td>`;
      }
      bodyHtml += `</tr>`;
    });
    body.innerHTML = bodyHtml;
  }
  async function cycleTeacherAttendance(element, teacherId, classId, dateStr) {
    const currentStatus = element.getAttribute("data-status") || "";
    let nextStatus = "";
    if (currentStatus === "") {
      nextStatus = "P";
    } else if (currentStatus === "P") {
      nextStatus = "V";
    } else if (currentStatus === "V") {
      nextStatus = "M";
    } else {
      nextStatus = "";
    }
    element.style.pointerEvents = "none";
    element.style.opacity = "0.5";
    try {
      await api("/api/teacher-attendance/single", {
        method: "PUT",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          class_id: classId,
          teacher_id: teacherId,
          date: dateStr,
          status: nextStatus ? nextStatus : null
        })
      });
      element.setAttribute("data-status", nextStatus);
      element.className = `teacher-att-cell ${nextStatus ? 'teacher-att-' + nextStatus : 'teacher-att-empty'} text-center`;
      element.textContent = nextStatus ? nextStatus : "-";
    } catch (err) {
      toast("Lỗi khi lưu điểm danh GV: " + err.message, true);
    } finally {
      element.style.pointerEvents = "auto";
      element.style.opacity = "1";
    }
  }
  // Khởi chạy
  async function boot() {
    await loadClasses();
    initFilters();
    // Khôi phục Tab
    const savedTab = localStorage.getItem("lastAttendanceTab");
    if (savedTab) {
      switchTab(savedTab);
    } else {
      switchTab("student");
    }
  }
  boot().catch(err => toast(err.message, true));
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
  let classes = [];
  let teachers = [];
  let dataTableInstance = null;
  function addTeacherAssignmentRow(assignment = null) {
    const container = document.getElementById("teacherAssignmentsContainer");
    const rowId = 'row_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.id = rowId;
    row.className = "grid grid-cols-12 gap-2 items-center border border-slate-100 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50/50 shadow-sm relative transition duration-150";
    const options = teachers.map(t => `<option value="${t.id}">${t.full_name}</option>`).join("");
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div class="col-span-4">
        <label class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 mb-0.5 block">Giáo viên</label>
        <select class="field !py-1 !px-2 text-sm rounded-lg border-slate-200 w-full assignment-teacher" required>
          <option value="">-- Chọn giáo viên --</option>
          ${options}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="col-span-2">
        <label class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 mb-0.5 block">Vai trò</label>
        <select class="field !py-1 !px-2 text-sm rounded-lg border-slate-200 w-full assignment-role">
          <option value="main">Chính</option>
          <option value="assistant">Phụ</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="col-span-3">
        <label class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 mb-0.5 block">Hình thức</label>
        <select class="field !py-1 !px-2 text-sm rounded-lg border-slate-200 w-full assignment-salary-type" onchange="onRowSalaryTypeChange(this)">
          <option value="fixed">Lương cứng</option>
          <option value="coefficient">Hệ số DT</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="col-span-2">
        <label class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 mb-0.5 block rate-label">Mức (đ)</label>
        <input type="number" step="any" min="0" class="field !py-1 !px-2 text-sm rounded-lg border-slate-200 w-full assignment-rate" required />
      </div>
      <div class="col-span-1 text-center pt-4">
        <button type="button" class="text-rose-500 hover:text-rose-700 transition" onclick="removeTeacherAssignmentRow('${rowId}')">
          <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5 mx-auto" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" />
          </svg>
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
    container.appendChild(row);
    if (assignment) {
      row.querySelector(".assignment-teacher").value = assignment.teacher_id;
      row.querySelector(".assignment-role").value = assignment.role;
      row.querySelector(".assignment-salary-type").value = assignment.salary_type;
      const rateInput = row.querySelector(".assignment-rate");
      if (assignment.salary_type === "fixed") {
        rateInput.value = assignment.fixed_salary_per_session;
      } else {
        rateInput.value = assignment.salary_coefficient;
      }
      onRowSalaryTypeChange(row.querySelector(".assignment-salary-type"));
    } else {
      row.querySelector(".assignment-rate").value = "450000";
    }
  }
  function removeTeacherAssignmentRow(rowId) {
    const row = document.getElementById(rowId);
    if (row) {
      row.remove();
    }
  }
  function onRowSalaryTypeChange(selectEl) {
    const row = selectEl.closest('.grid');
    const rateInput = row.querySelector('.assignment-rate');
    const rateLabel = row.querySelector('.rate-label');
    if (selectEl.value === 'fixed') {
      rateLabel.textContent = 'Mức (đ)';
      rateInput.placeholder = '450000';
      if (!rateInput.value || rateInput.value === '1' || rateInput.value === '1.0' || parseFloat(rateInput.value) <= 10) {
        rateInput.value = '450000';
      }
    } else {
      rateLabel.textContent = 'Hệ số';
      rateInput.placeholder = '1.0';
      if (!rateInput.value || rateInput.value === '450000' || parseFloat(rateInput.value) > 10) {
        rateInput.value = '1.0';
      }
    }
  }
  function openClassForm(item = null) {
    const form = document.getElementById("classForm");
    form.reset();
    form.elements.id.value = item?.id || "";
    form.elements.name.value = item?.name || "";
    form.elements.subject.value = item?.subject || "";
    form.elements.default_fee.value = item?.default_fee || 0;
    form.elements.notes.value = item?.notes || "";
    form.elements.is_active.checked = item ? item.is_active : true;
    const container = document.getElementById("teacherAssignmentsContainer");
    container.innerHTML = "";
    if (item && item.assignments && item.assignments.length > 0) {
      item.assignments.forEach(ass => addTeacherAssignmentRow(ass));
    } else if (item && item.teacher_id) {
      addTeacherAssignmentRow({
        teacher_id: item.teacher_id,
        role: "main",
        salary_type: item.salary_type || "fixed",
        fixed_salary_per_session: item.fixed_salary_per_session || 450000,
        salary_coefficient: item.salary_coefficient || 1.0
      });
    } else {
      addTeacherAssignmentRow();
    }
    setText("classFormTitle", item ? "Sửa lớp/môn học" : "Thêm lớp/môn học");
    document.getElementById("classModal").classList.remove("hidden");
  }
  function closeClassForm() { 
    document.getElementById("classModal").classList.add("hidden"); 
  }
  async function loadClasses() {
    classes = await api("/api/classes");
    if (dataTableInstance) {
      dataTableInstance.destroy();
    }
    document.getElementById("classRows").innerHTML = classes.map((item) => {
      let teachersDisplay = "";
      if (item.assignments && item.assignments.length > 0) {
        teachersDisplay = item.assignments.map(ass => {
          const t = teachers.find(x => x.id === ass.teacher_id);
          const tName = t ? t.full_name : `GV #${ass.teacher_id}`;
          const roleBadge = ass.role === "main" 
            ? `<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 rounded border border-teal-200 ml-1">Chính</span>`
            : `<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 rounded border border-slate-200 ml-1">Phụ</span>`;
          return `<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1 py-0.5">
            <span class="font-medium text-slate-700">${tName}</span>
            ${roleBadge}
          </div>`;
        }).join("");
      } else {
        const t = item.teacher_id ? teachers.find(x => x.id === item.teacher_id) : null;
        teachersDisplay = t 
          ? `<div class="flex items-center justify-between py-0.5">
               <span class="font-medium text-slate-700">${t.full_name}</span>
               <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 rounded border border-teal-200 ml-1">Chính (Cũ)</span>
             </div>`
          : `<span class="text-slate-400 text-xs">Chưa phân công</span>`;
      }
      let salaryDisplay = "";
      if (item.assignments && item.assignments.length > 0) {
        salaryDisplay = item.assignments.map(ass => {
          const t = teachers.find(x => x.id === ass.teacher_id);
          const tName = t ? t.full_name.split(" ").slice(-1)[0] : `GV`;
          const salStr = ass.salary_type === "fixed"
            ? `${formatMoney(ass.fixed_salary_per_session)}`
            : `Doanh thu (HS: ${ass.salary_coefficient})`;
          return `<div class="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-1">
            <span class="font-semibold">${tName}:</span> ${salStr}
          </div>`;
        }).join("");
      } else {
        const salStr = item.salary_type === "fixed"
          ? `${formatMoney(item.fixed_salary_per_session)}`
          : `Doanh thu (HS: ${item.salary_coefficient})`;
        salaryDisplay = `<span class="text-xs text-slate-600 font-semibold">${salStr}</span>`;
      }
      return `
        <tr>
          <td class="font-bold text-teal-800">${item.name}</td>
          <td class="font-semibold text-slate-800">${item.subject}</td>
          <td>${teachersDisplay}</td>
          <td class="font-bold text-slate-700">${formatMoney(item.default_fee)}</td>
          <td>${salaryDisplay}</td>
          <td>
            <span class="status-pill ${item.is_active ? "status-active" : "status-muted"}">
              ${item.is_active ? "Đang dạy" : "Ngưng"}
            </span>
          </td>
          <td class="space-x-1 whitespace-nowrap">
            <button class="btn btn-muted !min-height-0 !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold" onclick='openClassForm(classes.find(x=>x.id===${item.id}))'>Sửa</button>
            <button class="btn btn-danger !min-height-0 !py-1 !px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold" onclick="deleteClass(${item.id})">Ngưng</button>
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join("");
    dataTableInstance = $("#classesTable").DataTable({
      language: {
        url: "https://cdn.datatables.net/plug-ins/1.13.7/i18n/vi.json"
      },
      pageLength: 10,
      order: [[0, "asc"]],
      retrieve: true
    });
  }
  async function deleteClass(id) {
    if (!requireConfirm("Bạn có chắc chắn muốn ngưng hoạt động lớp/môn học này?")) return;
    await api(`/api/classes/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    toast("Đã cập nhật trạng thái lớp/môn học.");
    loadClasses();
  }
  document.getElementById("classForm").addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const data = formDataJson(event.target);
    const id = data.id;
    delete data.id;
    data.default_fee = parseInt(data.default_fee) || 0;
    const assignments = [];
    const container = document.getElementById("teacherAssignmentsContainer");
    const rows = container.querySelectorAll(".grid");
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const teacherIdVal = row.querySelector(".assignment-teacher").value;
      if (!teacherIdVal) return;
      const teacher_id = parseInt(teacherIdVal);
      const role = row.querySelector(".assignment-role").value;
      const salary_type = row.querySelector(".assignment-salary-type").value;
      const rate = parseFloat(row.querySelector(".assignment-rate").value) || 0;
      const ass = {
        teacher_id,
        role,
        salary_type,
        fixed_salary_per_session: salary_type === "fixed" ? Math.round(rate) : 450000,
        salary_coefficient: salary_type === "coefficient" ? rate : 1.0
      };
      assignments.push(ass);
    });
    data.assignments = assignments;
    if (assignments.length > 0) {
      const mainAss = assignments.find(a => a.role === "main") || assignments[0];
      data.teacher_id = mainAss.teacher_id;
      data.salary_type = mainAss.salary_type;
      data.fixed_salary_per_session = mainAss.fixed_salary_per_session;
      data.salary_coefficient = mainAss.salary_coefficient;
    } else {
      data.teacher_id = null;
      data.salary_type = "fixed";
      data.fixed_salary_per_session = 450000;
      data.salary_coefficient = 1.0;
    }
    try {
      await api(id ? `/api/classes/${id}` : "/api/classes", { 
        method: id ? "PUT" : "POST", 
        body: JSON.stringify(data) 
      });
      closeClassForm();
      toast("Đã lưu thông tin lớp/môn học thành công.");
      loadClasses();
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  });
  async function loadTeachers() {
    try {
      teachers = await api("/api/teachers?active=true");
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Lỗi khi tải giáo viên:", err);
    }
  }
  async function init() {
    await loadTeachers();
    await loadClasses();
  }
  init().catch((err) => toast(err.message, true));
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block scripts %}
<script>
  const defaultMonth = Number("{{ today.month }}");
  const defaultYear = Number("{{ today.year }}");
  document.getElementById("month").value = defaultMonth;
  document.getElementById("year").value = defaultYear;
  let chart;
  async function loadDashboard() {
    const month = Number(document.getElementById("month").value);
    const year = Number(document.getElementById("year").value);
    // Tải thông tin thống kê tổng quan
    const summary = await api(`/api/dashboard/summary?month=${month}&year=${year}`);
    setText("mStudents", summary.students);
    setText("mClasses", summary.classes);
    setText("mSessions", summary.sessions);
    setText("mRevenue", formatMoney(summary.revenue));
    // Cập nhật link tải báo cáo
    document.getElementById("exportExcelBtn").href = `/api/dashboard/export-excel?month=${month}&year=${year}`;
    document.getElementById("exportPdfBtn").href = `/api/dashboard/export-pdf?month=${month}&year=${year}`;
    // Tải thông tin biểu đồ doanh thu năm
    const revenue = await api(`/api/dashboard/revenue?year=${year}`);
    const labels = revenue.map((r) => `Tháng ${r.month}`);
    const data = revenue.map((r) => r.amount);
    if (chart) chart.destroy();
    chart = new Chart(document.getElementById("revenueChart"), {
      type: "bar",
      data: { 
        labels, 
        datasets: [{ 
          label: "Doanh thu", 
          data, 
          backgroundColor: "#0f766e", 
          borderRadius: 6 
        }] 
      },
      options: { 
        maintainAspectRatio: false, 
        plugins: { 
          legend: { display: false } 
        }, 
        scales: { 
          y: { 
            ticks: { callback: formatMoney } 
          } 
        } 
      },
    });
  }
  document.getElementById("month").addEventListener("change", loadDashboard);
  document.getElementById("year").addEventListener("change", loadDashboard);
  loadDashboard().catch((err) => toast(err.message, true));
</script>
{% endblock %}

  <body class="min-h-screen grid place-items-center bg-[#f6f8fb] p-6">
    <section class="panel w-full max-w-md p-8">
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-extrabold">Đăng nhập quản trị</h1>
      <p class="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Tài khoản mặc định lần đầu: admin / 123456</p>
      <form id="loginForm" class="mt-6 space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label class="label">Tên đăng nhập</label>
          <input class="field" name="username" placeholder="Nhập tên đăng nhập" autocomplete="username" required />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label class="label">Mật khẩu</label>
          <input class="field" name="password" type="password" placeholder="Nhập mật khẩu" autocomplete="current-password" required />
        </div>
        <button class="btn btn-primary w-full" type="submit">Đăng nhập</button>
      </form>
      <p id="loginError" class="mt-4 hidden rounded-lg bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm font-semibold text-red-700"></p>
    </section>
    <script src="/static/js/app.js"></script>
    <script>
      document.getElementById("loginForm").addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        const error = document.getElementById("loginError");
        error.classList.add("hidden");
        try {
          await api("/api/auth/login", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(formDataJson(event.target)) });
          window.location.href = "/dashboard";
        } catch (err) {
          error.textContent = err.message;
          error.classList.remove("hidden");
        }
      });
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

  <body>
    <!-- Lặp qua danh sách toàn bộ phiếu thu cần in -->

                <tbody>
                  {% for item in record.items %}
                  <tr>
                    <td class="subject">{{ item.subject }}</td>
                    <td>{{ item.sessions }}</td>
                    <td>{{ item.unit_fee | currency }}</td>
                    <td>{{ item.amount | currency }}</td>
                    <td>{{ item.notes or "" }}</td>
                  </tr>
                  {% endfor %}
                  <tr>
                    <td colspan="3">Tổng tiền (VNĐ) HỌC PHÍ</td>
                    <td><strong>{{ record.total_amount | currency }}</strong></td>
                    <td></td>
                  </tr>
                  <tr>
                    <td colspan="3"><strong>SỐ TIỀN PHẢI NỘP (VNĐ)</strong></td>
                    <td><strong>{{ record.total_amount | currency }}</strong></td>
                    <td></td>
                  </tr>
                </tbody>
              </table>
              <div class="payment-note">
                <div>{{ settings.payment_deadline }}</div>
                <div style="margin-top: 4px">
                  <span style="display: inline-block; width: 180px">Nội dung chuyển khoản:</span>
                  <span class="payment-content">{{ get_payment_content(record) }}</span>
                </div>
                <div style="margin-top: 4px">{{ settings.receipt_footer | safe }}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="right">
              <img class="qr" src="{{ qr_path }}" alt="Mã QR thanh toán" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    {% endfor %}
  </body>
</html>
